- **steadyspec-adopt**: Bootstrap or operate a proof-gated change workflow. - **steadyspec-apply**: Implement delegated work using existing code and behavior-focused checks. - **steadyspec-apply-flow**: Implement a recorded SteadySpec change with governed source binding, semantic correction, and behavior-focused verification. - **steadyspec-archive**: Archive or close an SDD change after review and doc sync. - **steadyspec-archive-flow**: SteadySpec archive verb. Triggers on `/steadyspec:archive &lt;change-id&gt;` and on user phrases like "close this change", "archive 099", "wrap up the change". The change-id is the substrate's id for the change to archive. ([BestBlade/SteadySpec](https://github.com/BestBlade/SteadySpec/tree/HEAD/en/flows/steadyspec-archive-flow) · ★1)
- **steadyspec-context-archaeology**: Reconstruct the bounded current-system path and relevant project history before an SDD decision. Use for full-governed proposals, when code looks strange, old behavior may encode past constraints, a change would delete or replace existing structure, or the agent must avoid pretending to understand why the system works this way. ([BestBlade/SteadySpec](https://github.com/BestBlade/SteadySpec/tree/HEAD/en/primitives/steadyspec-context-archaeology) · ★1)
- **steadyspec-debate**: Run a Proposer/Challenger debate for SDD decisions. - **steadyspec-doc-sync**: Sync durable docs after SDD implementation and review. ([BestBlade/SteadySpec](https://github.com/BestBlade/SteadySpec/tree/HEAD/en/primitives/steadyspec-doc-sync) · ★1)
- **steadyspec-explore**: Explore and clarify intent before an SDD change is proposed. - **steadyspec-explore-flow**: SteadySpec activation entry. Two modes - status (no topic) reports project state with attention-ranked decisions and optional handoff snapshot; topical (with topic) explores an idea with project history, prior decisions, likely owners, and high-risk triggers loaded. Triggers on `/steadyspec:explore [topic]` and on user phrases like "what's the project state", "where are we", "what's next", "let's explore X", "I'm thinking about Y". Activates SteadySpec for the session - once invoked, the agent stays spec-aware and reaches for the right `steadyspec-*` skill at later transition points. - **steadyspec-propose**: Create SDD proposal artifacts from clear intent. - **steadyspec-propose-flow**: SteadySpec propose verb. Writes a proposal artifact with hardened intent, decision ledger, risk routing, attention report, and traceability to prior changes. Triggers on `/steadyspec:propose &lt;intent&gt;` and on user phrases like "let's write a proposal", "write the spec", "create the change", "let's commit to this plan". - **steadyspec-review-against-intent**: Review completed SDD implementation against recorded intent before archive. - **steadyspec-verify-flow**: Verify a recorded SteadySpec change against authorized intent and current evidence before handoff or archive. - **steadyspec-workflow**: Choose and route the appropriate SteadySpec path for delegated software work.
